function bap_2fa_save_profile_fields( $user_id ) {
    if ( ! current_user_can( 'edit_user', $user_id ) || ! isset( $_POST['bap_2fa_user_method'] ) ) {
        return;
    }
    if ( ! empty( $_POST['bap_2fa_revoke_devices'] ) ) {
        delete_user_meta( $user_id, '_bap_2fa_trusted_devices' );
        if ( get_current_user_id() === (int) $user_id && ! headers_sent() ) {
            setcookie( 'bap_2fa_trust_' . $user_id, '', time() - 3600, COOKIEPATH, COOKIE_DOMAIN, is_ssl(), true );
        }
    }

    $chosen = sanitize_key( $_POST['bap_2fa_user_method'] );
    $secret = (string) get_user_meta( $user_id, '_bap_2fa_totp_secret', true );

    if ( 'totp' === $chosen ) {
        $current_method = get_user_meta( $user_id, '_bap_2fa_method', true );
        $verify_code    = isset( $_POST['bap_2fa_verify_setup'] ) ? sanitize_text_field( wp_unslash( $_POST['bap_2fa_verify_setup'] ) ) : '';
        if ( 'totp' !== $current_method && ! bap_2fa_verify_totp( $secret, $verify_code ) ) {
            return;
        }
        update_user_meta( $user_id, '_bap_2fa_method', 'totp' );
    } elseif ( 'email' === $chosen ) {
        update_user_meta( $user_id, '_bap_2fa_method', 'email' );
    } else {
        delete_user_meta( $user_id, '_bap_2fa_method' );
    }

    $existing = get_user_meta( $user_id, '_bap_2fa_backup_codes', true );
    if ( ! empty( $_POST['bap_2fa_regen_backups'] ) || ( in_array( $chosen, [ 'totp', 'email' ], true ) && empty( $existing ) ) ) {
        $codes = bap_2fa_generate_backup_codes( $user_id );
        set_transient( 'bap_2fa_new_backups_' . $user_id, $codes, 120 );
    }
}

add_action( 'edit_user_profile_update', 'bap_2fa_save_profile_fields' );

function bap_handle_web_rule_actions() {
    if ( ! current_user_can( 'manage_options' ) ) {
        return [ 'notice' => '', 'selected' => '__all__', 'rules' => bap_get_web_rules() ];
    }

    $rules    = bap_get_web_rules();
    $selected = isset( $_GET['rule_scope'] ) ? sanitize_text_field( wp_unslash( $_GET['rule_scope'] ) ) : '__all__';
    $notice   = '';

    if ( isset( $_POST['bap_add_web_url'] ) && check_admin_referer( 'bap_web_rules_nonce' ) ) {
        $new_url = trim( (string) wp_unslash( $_POST['bap_new_web_url'] ?? '' ) );
        if ( '' !== $new_url ) {
            $parsed_path = wp_parse_url( $new_url, PHP_URL_PATH );
            $clean_path  = '/' . ltrim( (string) ( $parsed_path ?: $new_url ), '/' );
            if ( ! isset( $rules[ $clean_path ] ) ) {
                $rules[ $clean_path ] = bap_get_default_web_rule( '1' );
                update_option( 'bap_web_rules', $rules );
            }
            $selected = $clean_path;
            $notice   = '<div class="notice notice-success is-dismissible"><p>Ãmbito <strong>' . esc_html( $clean_path ) . '</strong> aÃ±adido. Configura sus filtros abajo y pulsa Guardar.</p></div>';
        }
    }

    if ( isset( $_POST['bap_delete_web_url'] ) && check_admin_referer( 'bap_web_rules_nonce' ) ) {

            'block_firehol' => ( '1' === (string) ( $_POST['bap_rule_block_firehol'] ?? '0' ) ) ? '1' : '0',
            'redirect_url'  => esc_url_raw( trim( (string) wp_unslash( $_POST['bap_rule_redirect_url'] ?? '' ) ) ),
            'block_message' => sanitize_text_field( trim( (string) wp_unslash( $_POST['bap_rule_block_message'] ?? '' ) ) ),
        ] );

    }

        'notice'   => $notice,
        'selected' => $selected,
        'rules'    => $rules,
    ];
}

(function () {
    const list = document.getElementById('bap-conditions-list');
    const orderInput = document.getElementById('bap_rule_order');

    function syncOrderAndInterleave(cards) {
        if (!list) return;
        const switches = Array.from(list.querySelectorAll('.bap-logic-row'));
        list.innerHTML = '';
        cards.forEach((card, idx) => {
            list.appendChild(card);
            if (idx < switches.length) {
                list.appendChild(switches[idx]);
            }
        });
        if (orderInput) {
            orderInput.value = cards.map(c => c.dataset.key).join(',');
        }
    }

    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
        const toggleBtn = e.target.closest('.bap-logic-toggle');
        if (toggleBtn) {
            const wrap = toggleBtn.parentElement;
            const input = wrap.querySelector('input[type="hidden"]');
            const bg = toggleBtn.querySelector('.bap-sw-bg');
            const txt = toggleBtn.querySelector('.bap-sw-txt');
            const knob = toggleBtn.querySelector('.bap-sw-knob');
            const hint = wrap.querySelector('.bap-sw-hint');

            const nextIsOr = (input.value === 'AND');
            input.value = nextIsOr ? 'OR' : 'AND';
            bg.setAttribute('fill', nextIsOr ? '#d97706' : '#2271b1');
            txt.setAttribute('x', nextIsOr ? '27' : '47');
            txt.textContent = nextIsOr ? 'O' : 'Y';
            knob.setAttribute('cx', nextIsOr ? '58' : '16');
            hint.textContent = nextIsOr ? 'Opcional (alternativa al filtro anterior)' : 'Obligatorio (se exige ademÃ¡s del anterior)';
            return;
        }

        const upBtn = e.target.closest('.bap-move-up');
        const downBtn = e.target.closest('.bap-move-down');
        if ((upBtn || downBtn) && list) {
            const card = (upBtn || downBtn).closest('.bap-cond-card');
            const cards = Array.from(list.querySelectorAll('.bap-cond-card'));
            const idx = cards.indexOf(card);
            if (upBtn && idx > 0) {
                [cards[idx - 1], cards[idx]] = [cards[idx], cards[idx - 1]];
                syncOrderAndInterleave(cards);
            } else if (downBtn && idx < cards.length - 1) {
                [cards[idx], cards[idx + 1]] = [cards[idx + 1], cards[idx]];
                syncOrderAndInterleave(cards);
            }
        }
    });

    if (list) {
        let dragged = null;

        list.addEventListener('dragstart', function (e) {
            const card = e.target.closest('.bap-cond-card');
            if (!card) return;
            dragged = card;
            card.style.opacity = '0.5';
            e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
        });

        list.addEventListener('dragend', function () {
            if (dragged) {
                dragged.style.opacity = '';
                dragged = null;
            }
        });

        list.addEventListener('dragover', function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move';
        });

        list.addEventListener('drop', function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const target = e.target.closest('.bap-cond-card');
            if (!dragged || !target || dragged === target) return;

            const cards = Array.from(list.querySelectorAll('.bap-cond-card'));
            const fromIdx = cards.indexOf(dragged);
            const toIdx = cards.indexOf(target);
            cards.splice(fromIdx, 1);
            cards.splice(toIdx, 0, dragged);
            syncOrderAndInterleave(cards);
        });
    }
})();

function bap_get_abuseipdb_path() {
    return bap_get_geo_storage_path( 'abuseipdb-list.json' );

function bap_download_abuseipdb_database() {
    $api_key = trim( (string) get_option( 'bap_abuseipdb_key', '' ) );
    $map     = [];

    if ( '' !== $api_key ) {
        $url = 'https://' . implode( '.', [ 'api', 'abuseipdb', 'com' ] ) . '/api/v2/blacklist?confidenceMinimum=50&limit=10000';
        $res = wp_remote_get( $url, [
            'timeout' => 30,
            'headers' => [
                'Key'    => $api_key,
                'Accept' => 'application/json',
            ],
        ] );

        if ( is_wp_error( $res ) ) {
            return $res;
        }
        if ( 200 !== wp_remote_retrieve_response_code( $res ) ) {
            return new WP_Error( 'abuse_api', 'AbuseIPDB devolviÃ³ HTTP ' . wp_remote_retrieve_response_code( $res ) . '. Revisa tu API Key.' );
        }

        $body = json_decode( wp_remote_retrieve_body( $res ), true );
        $list = $body['data'] ?? [];
        foreach ( $list as $item ) {
            $ip    = $item['ipAddress'] ?? '';
            $score = (int) ( $item['abuseConfidenceScore'] ?? 0 );
            if ( filter_var( $ip, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP ) && $score > 0 ) {
                $map[ $ip ] = $score;
            }
        }
    } else {
        $mirror = 'https://' . implode( '.', [ 'raw', 'githubusercontent', 'com' ] ) . '/' . implode( '/', [ 'borestad', 'blocklist-abuseipdb', 'main', 'abuseipdb-s100-30d.ipv4' ] );
        $res    = wp_remote_get( $mirror, [ 'timeout' => 30 ] );
        if ( is_wp_error( $res ) || 200 !== wp_remote_retrieve_response_code( $res ) ) {
            return new WP_Error( 'abuse_mirror', 'No se pudo descargar el espejo pÃºblico ni hay API Key configurada.' );
        }
        $lines = preg_split( '/\r\n|\r|\n/', wp_remote_retrieve_body( $res ) );
        foreach ( $lines as $line ) {
            $ip = trim( strtok( $line, " \t#" ) );
            if ( filter_var( $ip, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP ) ) {
                $map[ $ip ] = 95;
            }
        }
    }

    if ( empty( $map ) ) {
        return new WP_Error( 'abuse_empty', 'La lista descargada no contiene IPs vÃ¡lidas.' );
    }

    return bap_save_json_list( 'abuseipdb-list.json', $map, 'bap_abuseipdb_last_updated', 'bap_abuseipdb_count' );
}

function bap_get_abuseipdb_score( $ip ) {
    if ( ! filter_var( $ip, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP ) ) {
        return null;
    }

    static $db = null;
    if ( null === $db ) {
        $file = bap_get_abuseipdb_path();
        $db   = file_exists( $file ) ? json_decode( (string) file_get_contents( $file ), true ) : [];
        if ( ! is_array( $db ) ) {
            $db = [];
        }
    }
    if ( isset( $db[ $ip ] ) ) {
        return (int) $db[ $ip ];
    }

    $tkey   = 'bap_abuse_' . md5( $ip );
    $cached = get_transient( $tkey );
    if ( false !== $cached ) {
        return (int) $cached;
    }

    $api_key = trim( (string) get_option( 'bap_abuseipdb_key', '' ) );
    if ( '' !== $api_key ) {
        $url = 'https://' . implode( '.', [ 'api', 'abuseipdb', 'com' ] ) . '/api/v2/check?ipAddress=' . rawurlencode( $ip ) . '&maxAgeInDays=90';
        $res = wp_remote_get( $url, [
            'timeout' => 4,
            'headers' => [ 'Key' => $api_key, 'Accept' => 'application/json' ],
        ] );
        if ( ! is_wp_error( $res ) && 200 === wp_remote_retrieve_response_code( $res ) ) {
            $data  = json_decode( wp_remote_retrieve_body( $res ), true );
            $score = (int) ( $data['data']['abuseConfidenceScore'] ?? 0 );
            set_transient( $tkey, $score, DAY_IN_SECONDS );
            return $score;
        }
    }

    return null;
}

function bap_install_abuseipdb_from_file( $tmp_path, $original_name ) {
    if ( '.gz' === strtolower( substr( $original_name, -3 ) ) ) {
        $tmp_unpacked = wp_tempnam( 'bap_abuse' );
        $dec          = bap_decompress_gz_to_file( $tmp_path, $tmp_unpacked );
        if ( is_wp_error( $dec ) ) {
            @unlink( $tmp_unpacked );
            return $dec;
        }
        $content = (string) @file_get_contents( $tmp_unpacked );
        @unlink( $tmp_unpacked );
    } else {
        $content = (string) @file_get_contents( $tmp_path );
    }

    if ( '' === $content ) {
        return new WP_Error( 'empty_file', 'El archivo subido estÃ¡ vacÃ­o.' );
    }

    $map  = [];
    $json = json_decode( $content, true );

    if ( is_array( $json ) ) {
        $items = $json['data'] ?? $json;
        foreach ( $items as $k => $v ) {
            if ( is_array( $v ) && ! empty( $v['ipAddress'] ) ) {
                $ip    = trim( $v['ipAddress'] );
                $score = (int) ( $v['abuseConfidenceScore'] ?? 95 );
                if ( filter_var( $ip, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP ) ) {
                    $map[ $ip ] = max( 1, min( 100, $score ) );
                }
            } elseif ( filter_var( $k, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP ) ) {
                $map[ $k ] = max( 1, min( 100, (int) $v ) );
            }
        }
    } else {
        $lines = preg_split( '/\r\n|\r|\n/', $content );
        foreach ( $lines as $line ) {
            $line = trim( $line );
            if ( '' === $line || '#' === $line[0] ) {
                continue;
            }
            $parts = preg_split( '/[\s,;]+/', $line );
            $ip    = trim( $parts[0] ?? '' );
            $score = isset( $parts[1] ) && is_numeric( $parts[1] ) ? (int) $parts[1] : 95;
            if ( filter_var( $ip, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP ) ) {
                $map[ $ip ] = max( 1, min( 100, $score ) );
            }
        }
    }

    if ( empty( $map ) ) {
        return new WP_Error( 'invalid_abuse', 'No se encontraron direcciones IP vÃ¡lidas en el archivo.' );
    }

    return bap_save_json_list( 'abuseipdb-list.json', $map, 'bap_abuseipdb_last_updated', 'bap_abuseipdb_count' );
}

<?php
if ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) {
    exit;
}

function bap_is_valid_ip_or_cidr( $entry ) {
    $entry = trim( (string) $entry );
    if ( '' === $entry ) {
        return false;
    }
    if ( false === strpos( $entry, '/' ) ) {
        return (bool) filter_var( $entry, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP );
    }
    list( $ip, $mask ) = explode( '/', $entry, 2 );
    if ( ! ctype_digit( $mask ) ) {
        return false;
    }
    $bits = (int) $mask;
    if ( filter_var( $ip, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP, FILTER_FLAG_IPV4 ) ) {
        return ( $bits >= 0 && $bits <= 32 );
    }
    if ( filter_var( $ip, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP, FILTER_FLAG_IPV6 ) ) {
        return ( $bits >= 0 && $bits <= 128 );
    }
    return false;
}

function bap_ip_in_list( $ip, $list ) {
    if ( empty( $list ) || ! filter_var( $ip, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP ) ) {
        return false;
    }
    if ( in_array( $ip, $list, true ) ) {
        return true;
    }

    $ip_bin = @inet_pton( $ip );
    if ( false === $ip_bin ) {
        return false;
    }
    $ip_len = strlen( $ip_bin );

    foreach ( $list as $entry ) {
        if ( false === strpos( $entry, '/' ) ) {
            continue;
        }
        list( $subnet, $mask ) = explode( '/', $entry, 2 );
        $sub_bin = @inet_pton( $subnet );
        if ( false === $sub_bin || strlen( $sub_bin ) !== $ip_len || ! ctype_digit( $mask ) ) {
            continue;
        }

        $bits = (int) $mask;
        $max  = $ip_len * 8;
        if ( $bits < 0 || $bits > $max ) {
            continue;
        }
        if ( 0 === $bits ) {
            return true;
        }

        $bytes = intdiv( $bits, 8 );
        $rem   = $bits % 8;

        if ( $bytes > 0 && substr( $ip_bin, 0, $bytes ) !== substr( $sub_bin, 0, $bytes ) ) {
            continue;
        }
        if ( 0 === $rem ) {
            return true;
        }
        $byte_mask = ( 0xFF << ( 8 - $rem ) ) & 0xFF;
        if ( ( ord( $ip_bin[ $bytes ] ) & $byte_mask ) === ( ord( $sub_bin[ $bytes ] ) & $byte_mask ) ) {
            return true;
        }
    }

    return false;
}

<?php

function bap_prepare_digest_stats( array $queue ) {
    $grouped    = [];
    $by_country = [];
    $by_asn     = [];
    $unique_ips = [];

    foreach ( $queue as $row ) {
        $ip      = (string) ( $row['ip'] ?? 'N/D' );
        $country = (string) ( $row['country'] ?? 'DESCONOCIDO' );
        $asn     = (string) ( $row['asn'] ?? 'N/D' );
        $risk    = (string) ( $row['risk'] ?? 'N/D' );
        $reason  = (string) ( $row['reason'] ?? 'Bloqueado' );
        $t       = (string) ( $row['time'] ?? '' );

        $unique_ips[ $ip ]      = true;
        $by_country[ $country ] = ( $by_country[ $country ] ?? 0 ) + 1;
        if ( '' !== $asn && 'N/D' !== $asn && 'Sin base de datos ASN' !== $asn ) {
            $by_asn[ $asn ] = ( $by_asn[ $asn ] ?? 0 ) + 1;
        }

        $key = $ip . '|' . $reason;
        if ( ! isset( $grouped[ $key ] ) ) {
            $grouped[ $key ] = [
                'count'      => 0,
                'first_time' => $t,
                'last_time'  => $t,
                'ip'         => $ip,
                'country'    => $country,
                'asn'        => $asn,
                'risk'       => $risk,
                'reason'     => $reason,
            ];
        }
        $grouped[ $key ]['count']++;
        if ( '' !== $t ) {
            if ( '' === $grouped[ $key ]['first_time'] || $t < $grouped[ $key ]['first_time'] ) {
                $grouped[ $key ]['first_time'] = $t;
            }
            if ( '' === $grouped[ $key ]['last_time'] || $t > $grouped[ $key ]['last_time'] ) {
                $grouped[ $key ]['last_time'] = $t;
            }
        }
    }

    uasort( $grouped, fn( $a, $b ) => $b['count'] <=> $a['count'] );
    arsort( $by_country );
    arsort( $by_asn );

    return [
        'total_hits'    => count( $queue ),
        'unique_ips'    => count( $unique_ips ),
        'grouped'       => $grouped,
        'top_countries' => array_slice( $by_country, 0, 5, true ),
        'top_asns'      => array_slice( $by_asn, 0, 5, true ),
    ];
}

function bap_encrypt_ext_pass( $plain ) {
    if ( '' === (string) $plain ) {
        return '';
    }
    if ( function_exists( 'openssl_encrypt' ) ) {
        $key = hash( 'sha256', AUTH_SALT, true );
        $iv  = random_bytes( 16 );
        $enc = openssl_encrypt( $plain, 'aes-256-cbc', $key, OPENSSL_RAW_DATA, $iv );
        if ( false !== $enc ) {
            return 'enc:' . base64_encode( $iv . $enc );
        }
    }
    return $plain;
}

function bap_decrypt_ext_pass( $stored ) {
    $stored = (string) $stored;
    if ( 0 === strpos( $stored, 'enc:' ) && function_exists( 'openssl_decrypt' ) ) {
        $raw = base64_decode( substr( $stored, 4 ), true );
        if ( false !== $raw && strlen( $raw ) > 16 ) {
            $key = hash( 'sha256', AUTH_SALT, true );
            $dec = openssl_decrypt( substr( $raw, 16 ), 'aes-256-cbc', $key, OPENSSL_RAW_DATA, substr( $raw, 0, 16 ) );
            if ( false !== $dec ) {
                return $dec;
            }
        }
    }
    return $stored;
}

function bap_get_external_wpdb( &$error_msg = '' ) {
    if ( '1' !== (string) get_option( 'bap_ext_db_enabled', '0' ) ) {
        return null;
    }

    $host = trim( (string) get_option( 'bap_ext_db_host', '' ) );
    $port = (int) get_option( 'bap_ext_db_port', 3306 );
    $name = trim( (string) get_option( 'bap_ext_db_name', '' ) );
    $user = trim( (string) get_option( 'bap_ext_db_user', '' ) );
    $pass = bap_decrypt_ext_pass( get_option( 'bap_ext_db_pass', '' ) );

    if ( '' === $host || '' === $name || '' === $user ) {
        $error_msg = 'Faltan datos obligatorios (Host, Base de Datos o Usuario).';
        return null;
    }

    $db_host = ( $port > 0 && 3306 !== $port ) ? $host . ':' . $port : $host;

    static $ext_db = null;
    if ( null === $ext_db ) {
        $ext_db = new wpdb( $user, $pass, $name, $db_host );
        $ext_db->hide_errors();
    }

    if ( ! empty( $ext_db->error ) || ! $ext_db->dbh ) {
        $error_msg = is_wp_error( $ext_db->error ) ? $ext_db->error->get_error_message() : 'No se pudo conectar al servidor MySQL remoto.';
        $ext_db    = null;
        return null;
    }

    return $ext_db;
}

function bap_ensure_external_logs_table( $ext_db, $table ) {
    $safe_table = preg_replace( '/[^a-zA-Z0-9_]/', '', (string) $table );
    if ( '' === $safe_table ) {
        $safe_table = 'bap_global_blocked_logs';
    }

    $sql = "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `{$safe_table}` (
        `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
        `site_domain` varchar(190) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
        `ip_address` varchar(45) NOT NULL,
        `country_code` varchar(16) NOT NULL,
        `asn` varchar(190) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
        `block_type` varchar(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'admin',
        `reason` varchar(255) NOT NULL,
        `request_uri` text NOT NULL,
        `user_agent` text NOT NULL,
        `created_at` datetime NOT NULL,
        PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
        KEY `idx_created_at` (`created_at`),
        KEY `idx_ip_address` (`ip_address`),
        KEY `idx_site_domain` (`site_domain`)
    ) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;";

    $res = $ext_db->query( $sql );
    if ( false !== $res ) {
        $has_ua = $ext_db->get_var( "SHOW COLUMNS FROM `{$safe_table}` LIKE 'user_agent'" );
        if ( ! $has_ua ) {
            $ext_db->query( "ALTER TABLE `{$safe_table}` ADD COLUMN `user_agent` text NOT NULL AFTER `request_uri`" );
        }
    }
    return ( false !== $res );
}

function bap_log_to_external_db( $ip, $country, $asn, $type, $reason, $uri, $now, $user_agent = '' ) {
    $err    = '';
    $ext_db = bap_get_external_wpdb( $err );
    if ( ! $ext_db ) {
        return false;
    }

    $table = preg_replace( '/[^a-zA-Z0-9_]/', '', (string) get_option( 'bap_ext_db_table', 'bap_global_blocked_logs' ) );
    if ( '' === $table ) {
        $table = 'bap_global_blocked_logs';
    }

    static $table_checked = false;
    if ( ! $table_checked ) {
        bap_ensure_external_logs_table( $ext_db, $table );
        $table_checked = true;
    }

    $domain = (string) wp_parse_url( home_url(), PHP_URL_HOST );

    $inserted = $ext_db->insert(
        $table,
        [
            'site_domain'  => $domain,
            'ip_address'   => $ip,
            'country_code' => $country,
            'asn'          => $asn,
            'block_type'   => $type,
            'reason'       => $reason,
            'request_uri'  => $uri,
            'user_agent'   => $user_agent,
            'created_at'   => $now,
        ],
        [ '%s', '%s', '%s', '%s', '%s', '%s', '%s', '%s', '%s' ]
    );

    return ( false !== $inserted );
}

function bap_get_geo_storage_path( $filename ) {
    $upload = wp_upload_dir();
    $dir    = trailingslashit( $upload['basedir'] ) . 'bap-geo';
    if ( ! file_exists( $dir ) ) {
        wp_mkdir_p( $dir );
        @file_put_contents( $dir . '/.htaccess', "Deny from all\n" );
        @file_put_contents( $dir . '/index.php', "<?php // Silence\n" );
    }
    return $dir . '/' . ltrim( (string) $filename, '/' );
}

function bap_save_json_list( $filename, $data, $date_opt, $count_opt = '' ) {
    $path = bap_get_geo_storage_path( $filename );
    if ( false === file_put_contents( $path, wp_json_encode( $data ) ) ) {
        return new WP_Error( 'write_fail', 'No se pudo escribir en ' . esc_html( $filename ) . '. Revisa los permisos de wp-content/uploads/bap-geo/.' );
    }
    update_option( $date_opt, current_time( 'mysql' ) );
    if ( '' !== $count_opt && is_array( $data ) ) {
        update_option( $count_opt, count( $data ) );
    }
    return true;
}

function bap_decompress_gz_to_file( $src_gz, $dest_file ) {
    if ( ! function_exists( 'gzopen' ) ) {
        return new WP_Error( 'no_zlib', 'PHP no tiene activa la extensiÃ³n zlib (gzopen).' );
    }
    $gz  = @gzopen( $src_gz, 'rb' );
    $out = @fopen( $dest_file, 'wb' );
    if ( ! $gz || ! $out ) {
        if ( $gz ) {
            gzclose( $gz );
        }
        return new WP_Error( 'gz_fail', 'Error al descomprimir el archivo .gz.' );
    }
    while ( ! gzeof( $gz ) ) {
        fwrite( $out, gzread( $gz, 65536 ) );
    }
    gzclose( $gz );
    fclose( $out );
    return true;
}

function bap_send_download_headers( $filename, $content_type ) {
    while ( ob_get_level() > 0 ) {
        ob_end_clean();
    }
    nocache_headers();
    header( 'Content-Type: ' . $content_type . '; charset=utf-8' );
    header( 'Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="' . sanitize_file_name( $filename ) . '"' );
}

function bap_get_mmdb_path() {
    return bap_get_geo_storage_path( 'dbip-lite.mmdb' );
}

function bap_get_asn_mmdb_path() {
    return bap_get_geo_storage_path( 'dbip-asn.mmdb' );
}

function bap_verify_and_save_mmdb( $tmp_file, $is_gz, $type = 'country' ) {
    $dest = ( 'asn' === $type ) ? bap_get_asn_mmdb_path() : bap_get_mmdb_path();
    $tmp  = $dest . '.tmp';

    if ( $is_gz ) {
        $dec = bap_decompress_gz_to_file( $tmp_file, $tmp );
        if ( is_wp_error( $dec ) ) {
            return $dec;
        }
    } elseif ( ! @copy( $tmp_file, $tmp ) ) {
        return new WP_Error( 'copy_fail', 'No se pudo copiar el archivo.' );
    }

    try {
        $test = new BAP_MMDB_Reader( $tmp );
        if ( 'asn' === $type ) {
            $check = $test->lookup_asn( '8.8.8.8' );
            unset( $test );
            if ( false === strpos( $check, 'AS15169' ) ) {
                @unlink( $tmp );
                return new WP_Error( 'verify_fail', 'El archivo no es una base de datos ASN vÃ¡lida.' );
            }
        } else {
            $code = $test->lookup_country( '8.8.8.8' );
            unset( $test );
            if ( 'US' !== $code ) {
                @unlink( $tmp );
                return new WP_Error( 'verify_fail', 'El archivo no es una base de datos de PaÃ­ses vÃ¡lida.' );
            }
        }
    } catch ( Exception $e ) {
        @unlink( $tmp );
        return new WP_Error( 'parse_fail', $e->getMessage() );
    }

    if ( ! @rename( $tmp, $dest ) ) {
        @unlink( $tmp );
        return new WP_Error( 'write_fail', 'No se pudo guardar la base de datos .mmdb en disco.' );
    }
    $opt_key = ( 'asn' === $type ) ? 'bap_asn_mmdb_last_updated' : 'bap_mmdb_last_updated';
    update_option( $opt_key, current_time( 'mysql' ) );
    return true;
}

function bap_download_mmdb_database( $type = 'country' ) {
    $ym   = gmdate( 'Y-m' );
    $prev = gmdate( 'Y-m', strtotime( '-1 month' ) );

    if ( 'asn' === $type ) {
        $urls = [
            'https://' . implode( '.', [ 'raw', 'githubusercontent', 'com' ] ) . '/' . implode( '/', [ 'sapics', 'ip-location-db', 'main', 'dbip-asn', 'dbip-asn.mmdb.gz' ] ),
            'https://' . implode( '.', [ 'download', 'db-ip', 'com' ] ) . '/' . implode( '/', [ 'free', 'dbip-asn-lite-' . $ym . '.mmdb.gz' ] ),
            'https://' . implode( '.', [ 'download', 'db-ip', 'com' ] ) . '/' . implode( '/', [ 'free', 'dbip-asn-lite-' . $prev . '.mmdb.gz' ] ),
        ];
    } else {
        $urls = [
            'https://' . implode( '.', [ 'raw', 'githubusercontent', 'com' ] ) . '/' . implode( '/', [ 'sapics', 'ip-location-db', 'main', 'dbip-country', 'dbip-country.mmdb.gz' ] ),
            'https://' . implode( '.', [ 'download', 'db-ip', 'com' ] ) . '/' . implode( '/', [ 'free', 'dbip-country-lite-' . $ym . '.mmdb.gz' ] ),
            'https://' . implode( '.', [ 'download', 'db-ip', 'com' ] ) . '/' . implode( '/', [ 'free', 'dbip-country-lite-' . $prev . '.mmdb.gz' ] ),
        ];
    }

    $dest   = ( 'asn' === $type ) ? bap_get_asn_mmdb_path() : bap_get_mmdb_path();
    $tmp_gz = $dest . '.tmp.gz';
    $errors = [];

    foreach ( $urls as $url ) {
        $res = wp_remote_get( $url, [ 'timeout' => 30, 'stream' => true, 'filename' => $tmp_gz ] );
        if ( ! is_wp_error( $res ) && 200 === wp_remote_retrieve_response_code( $res ) && file_exists( $tmp_gz ) && filesize( $tmp_gz ) > 100000 ) {
            $ok = bap_verify_and_save_mmdb( $tmp_gz, true, $type );
            @unlink( $tmp_gz );
            return $ok;
        }
        $errors[] = basename( $url ) . ': ' . ( is_wp_error( $res ) ? $res->get_error_message() : 'HTTP ' . wp_remote_retrieve_response_code( $res ) );
        @unlink( $tmp_gz );
    }
    return new WP_Error( 'dl_fail', implode( ' | ', $errors ) );
}

function bap_get_country_by_ip( $ip, &$source = '' ) {
    if ( ! filter_var( $ip, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP ) ) {
        return 'DESCONOCIDO';
    }
    $mmdb = bap_get_mmdb_path();
    if ( file_exists( $mmdb ) ) {
        try {
            static $reader = null;
            if ( null === $reader ) {
                $reader = new BAP_MMDB_Reader( $mmdb );
            }
            $code = $reader->lookup_country( $ip );
            if ( preg_match( '/^[A-Z]{2}$/i', $code ) ) {
                $source = 'Base de datos local (.mmdb)';
                return strtoupper( $code );
            }
        } catch ( Exception $e ) {}
    }
    $source = 'Sin base de datos local';
    return 'DESCONOCIDO';
}

function bap_get_asn_by_ip( $ip, &$asn_number = '' ) {
    $asn_number = '';
    if ( ! filter_var( $ip, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP ) ) {
        return 'IP invÃ¡lida';
    }

    $asn_mmdb = bap_get_asn_mmdb_path();
    if ( file_exists( $asn_mmdb ) ) {
        try {
            static $asn_reader = null;
            if ( null === $asn_reader ) {
                $asn_reader = new BAP_MMDB_Reader( $asn_mmdb );
            }
            $local_asn = $asn_reader->lookup_asn( $ip );
            if ( '' !== $local_asn ) {
                if ( preg_match( '/^(AS\d+)/i', $local_asn, $m ) ) {
                    $asn_number = strtoupper( reset( $m ) );
                }
                return $local_asn;
            }
        } catch ( Exception $e ) {}
    }

    $cache = get_option( 'bap_asn_cache', [] );
    if ( is_array( $cache ) && isset( $cache[ $ip ] ) ) {
        if ( preg_match( '/^(AS\d+)/i', $cache[ $ip ], $m ) ) {
            $asn_number = strtoupper( reset( $m ) );
        }
        return $cache[ $ip ];
    }

    return 'Sin base de datos ASN';
}

<?php
if ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) {
    exit;
}

define( 'BAP_GITHUB_REPO', 'ReinosDeLeyenda/bloqueo-admin-pais' );

function bap_get_github_latest_release() {
    $cache_key = 'bap_gh_release_' . md5( BAP_GITHUB_REPO );
    $cached    = get_transient( $cache_key );
    if ( false !== $cached ) {
        return $cached;
    }

    $host = 'api' . '.github.com';
    $url  = 'https://' . $host . '/repos/' . BAP_GITHUB_REPO . '/releases/latest';
    $res  = wp_remote_get( $url, [
        'timeout' => 10,
        'headers' => [
            'Accept'     => 'application/vnd.github.v3+json',
            'User-Agent' => 'WordPress-BAP-Updater',
        ],
    ] );

    if ( is_wp_error( $res ) || 200 !== wp_remote_retrieve_response_code( $res ) ) {
        return false;
    }

    $body = json_decode( wp_remote_retrieve_body( $res ), true );
    if ( empty( $body['tag_name'] ) ) {
        return false;
    }

    $zip_url = '';
    if ( ! empty( $body['assets'] ) && is_array( $body['assets'] ) ) {
        foreach ( $body['assets'] as $asset ) {
            if ( '.zip' === substr( (string) ( $asset['name'] ?? '' ), -4 ) ) {
                $zip_url = $asset['browser_download_url'] ?? '';
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    if ( '' === $zip_url ) {
        $zip_url = $body['zipball_url'] ?? '';
    }

    $info = [
        'version'   => ltrim( (string) $body['tag_name'], 'vV' ),
        'zip_url'   => $zip_url,
        'html_url'  => $body['html_url'] ?? '',
        'changelog' => $body['body'] ?? '',
        'published' => $body['published_at'] ?? '',
    ];

    set_transient( $cache_key, $info, 6 * HOUR_IN_SECONDS );
    return $info;
}

add_filter( 'pre_set_site_transient_update_plugins', function( $transient ) {
    if ( empty( $transient->checked ) ) {
        return $transient;
    }

    $plugin_slug = plugin_basename( dirname( __DIR__ ) . '/bloqueo-admin-pais.php' );
    $slug        = dirname( $plugin_slug );
    if ( '.' === $slug ) {
        $slug = basename( $plugin_slug, '.php' );
    }

    $current_ver = $transient->checked[ $plugin_slug ] ?? '1.0';
    $release     = bap_get_github_latest_release();

    if ( $release && ! empty( $release['zip_url'] ) && version_compare( $release['version'], $current_ver, '>' ) ) {
        $transient->response[ $plugin_slug ] = (object) [
            'slug'        => $slug,
            'plugin'      => $plugin_slug,
            'new_version' => $release['version'],
            'url'         => $release['html_url'],
            'package'     => $release['zip_url'],
        ];
    }

    return $transient;
});

add_filter( 'plugins_api', function( $res, $action, $args ) {
    $plugin_folder = basename( dirname( __DIR__ ) );
    if ( 'plugin_information' !== $action || ( $args->slug ?? '' ) !== $plugin_folder ) {
        return $res;
    }

    $release = bap_get_github_latest_release();
    if ( ! $release ) {
        return $res;
    }

    return (object) [
        'name'          => 'Bloqueo Admin por PaÃ­s e IP',
        'slug'          => $plugin_folder,
        'version'       => $release['version'],
        'homepage'      => $release['html_url'],
        'download_link' => $release['zip_url'],
        'last_updated'  => $release['published'],
        'sections'      => [
            'changelog' => nl2br( esc_html( $release['changelog'] ) ),
        ],
    ];
}, 10, 3 );

add_filter( 'upgrader_source_selection', function( $source, $remote_source, $upgrader, $hook_extra ) {
    $plugin_slug = plugin_basename( dirname( __DIR__ ) . '/bloqueo-admin-pais.php' );
    if ( ( $hook_extra['plugin'] ?? '' ) !== $plugin_slug ) {
        return $source;
    }
    $desired = trailingslashit( $remote_source ) . dirname( $plugin_slug );
    if ( untrailingslashit( $source ) !== $desired ) {
        global $wp_filesystem;
        if ( $wp_filesystem && $wp_filesystem->move( $source, $desired ) ) {
            return trailingslashit( $desired );
        }
    }
    return $source;
}, 10, 4 );

add_action( 'delete_site_transient_update_plugins', function() {
    delete_transient( 'bap_gh_release_' . md5( BAP_GITHUB_REPO ) );
});

// 1. AÃ±adir enlace "Buscar actualizaciones" en la pantalla de Plugins
add_filter( 'plugin_action_links_' . plugin_basename( dirname( __DIR__ ) . '/bloqueo-admin-pais.php' ), function( $links ) {
    $url = wp_nonce_url(
        admin_url( 'plugins.php?bap_force_update_check=1' ),
        'bap_force_update_nonce'
    );

    return $links;
} );

<?php
if ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) {
    exit;

function bap_get_default_protected_paths() {
    return implode( "\n", [
        '/wp-admin/*', '/wp-login.php', '/wp-register.php', '/xmlrpc.php', '/wp-json/wp/v2/users*',
        '*.log', '*.zip', '*.rar', '*.tar', '*.tar.gz', '*.tgz', '*.7z', '*.gz', '*.bak', '*.sql', '*.env', '*.git*',
    ] );
}

function bap_is_protected_request() {
    $raw_paths = get_option( 'bap_protected_paths', null );
    if ( null === $raw_paths ) {
        $raw_paths = bap_get_default_protected_paths();
    }

    $patterns = bap_parse_list( $raw_paths, false );
    if ( empty( $patterns ) ) {
        return false;
    }

    $uri  = isset( $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] ) ? strtolower( rawurldecode( wp_unslash( $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] ) ) ) : '';
    $path = (string) wp_parse_url( $uri, PHP_URL_PATH );
    if ( '' === $path ) {
        $path = $uri;
    }

    if ( false !== strpos( $path, '/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php' ) || false !== strpos( $path, '/wp-admin/admin-post.php' ) ) {
        return false;
    }

    foreach ( $patterns as $pattern ) {
        $pat = strtolower( trim( $pattern ) );
        if ( '' === $pat ) {
            continue;
        }
        $regex = '#^' . str_replace( '\*', '.*', preg_quote( $pat, '#' ) ) . '$#i';
        if ( preg_match( $regex, $path ) || preg_match( $regex, $uri ) ) {
            return true;
        }
        if ( '/*' === substr( $pat, -2 ) && rtrim( $path, '/' ) === substr( $pat, 0, -2 ) ) {
            return true;
        }
    }

    return false;
}

function bap_sync_htaccess_rules() {
    if ( ! function_exists( 'insert_with_markers' ) ) {
        require_once ABSPATH . 'wp-admin/includes/misc.php';
    }

    $htaccess_file = ABSPATH . '.htaccess';
    if ( ! file_exists( $htaccess_file ) || ! is_writable( $htaccess_file ) ) {
        return false;
    }

    if ( '1' !== (string) get_option( 'bap_enable_htaccess', '0' ) ) {
        return insert_with_markers( $htaccess_file, 'Bloqueo Admin Pais', [] );
    }

    $raw_paths = get_option( 'bap_protected_paths', null );
    if ( null === $raw_paths ) {
        $raw_paths = bap_get_default_protected_paths();
    }

    $exts = [];
    foreach ( bap_parse_list( $raw_paths, false ) as $pat ) {
        if ( preg_match( '/^\*\.([a-zA-Z0-9._-]+)$/', $pat, $m ) ) {
            $clean_ext = str_replace( '*', '', $m[1] );
            if ( '' !== $clean_ext ) {
                $exts[] = preg_quote( $clean_ext, '/' );
            }
        }
    }

    $block_xmlrpc = ( '1' === (string) get_option( 'bap_block_xmlrpc', '1' ) );
    $rules        = [];

    if ( ! empty( $exts ) || $block_xmlrpc ) {
        $secret = trim( (string) get_option( 'bap_secret_key', '' ) );
        $cname  = '' !== $secret ? 'bap_bypass_' . md5( $secret ) : '';

        $rules[] = '<IfModule mod_rewrite.c>';
        $rules[] = 'RewriteEngine On';

        if ( ! empty( $exts ) ) {
            if ( '' !== $cname ) {
                $rules[] = 'RewriteCond %{HTTP_COOKIE} !' . $cname . '= [NC]';
            }
            $rules[] = 'RewriteCond %{REQUEST_URI} \.(' . implode( '|', array_unique( $exts ) ) . ')$ [NC]';
            $rules[] = 'RewriteRule .* - [F,L]';
        }

        if ( $block_xmlrpc ) {
            if ( '' !== $cname ) {
                $rules[] = 'RewriteCond %{HTTP_COOKIE} !' . $cname . '= [NC]';
            }
            $rules[] = 'RewriteRule ^xmlrpc\.php$ - [F,L]';
        }

        $rules[] = '</IfModule>';
    }

    return insert_with_markers( $htaccess_file, 'Bloqueo Admin Pais', $rules );
}

function bap_get_logs_table_name() {
    global $wpdb;
    return $wpdb->prefix . 'bap_blocked_logs';
}

function bap_maybe_create_logs_table() {
    if ( '1.2' === get_option( 'bap_logs_table_ver' ) ) {
        return;
    }
    global $wpdb;
    $table   = bap_get_logs_table_name();
    $charset = $wpdb->get_charset_collate();

    $sql = "CREATE TABLE `{$table}` (
        id bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
        ip_address varchar(45) NOT NULL,
        country_code varchar(16) NOT NULL,
        asn varchar(190) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
        block_type varchar(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'admin',
        reason varchar(255) NOT NULL,
        request_uri text NOT NULL,
        user_agent text NOT NULL,
        created_at datetime NOT NULL,
        PRIMARY KEY (id),
        KEY idx_created_at (created_at),
        KEY idx_ip_address (ip_address)
    ) {$charset};";

    require_once ABSPATH . 'wp-admin/includes/upgrade.php';
    dbDelta( $sql );
    update_option( 'bap_logs_table_ver', '1.2' );
}
add_action( 'admin_init', 'bap_maybe_create_logs_table' );

function bap_get_sanitized_user_agent() {
    if ( empty( $_SERVER['HTTP_USER_AGENT'] ) ) {
        return '';
    }
    return mb_substr( sanitize_text_field( wp_unslash( $_SERVER['HTTP_USER_AGENT'] ) ), 0, 500 );
}

function bap_log_blocked_attempt( $ip, $country, $reason ) {
    global $wpdb;
    bap_maybe_create_logs_table();

    $uri        = isset( $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] ) ? esc_url_raw( wp_unslash( $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] ) ) : '/';
    $asn        = function_exists( 'bap_get_asn_by_ip' ) ? bap_get_asn_by_ip( $ip ) : '';
    $type       = ( 0 === strpos( $reason, 'Web:' ) ) ? 'web' : 'admin';
    $user_agent = bap_get_sanitized_user_agent();
    $now        = current_time( 'mysql' );

    $logged_externally = false;
    if ( function_exists( 'bap_log_to_external_db' ) ) {
        $logged_externally = bap_log_to_external_db( $ip, $country, $asn, $type, $reason, $uri, $now, $user_agent );
    }

    if ( ! $logged_externally ) {
        $wpdb->insert(
            bap_get_logs_table_name(),
            [
                'ip_address'   => $ip,
                'country_code' => $country,
                'asn'          => $asn,
                'block_type'   => $type,
                'reason'       => $reason,
                'request_uri'  => $uri,
                'user_agent'   => $user_agent,
                'created_at'   => $now,
            ],
            [ '%s', '%s', '%s', '%s', '%s', '%s', '%s', '%s' ]
        );
    }

    $max_attempts = (int) get_option( 'bap_auto_ban_attempts', 0 );
    if ( $max_attempts > 0 && ! bap_is_server_ip( $ip ) ) {
        $key      = 'bap_att_' . md5( $ip );
        $attempts = (int) get_transient( $key ) + 1;
        set_transient( $key, $attempts, HOUR_IN_SECONDS );

        if ( $attempts >= $max_attempts ) {
            $blacklist = bap_parse_list( get_option( 'bap_blacklist_ips', '' ), false );
            if ( ! in_array( $ip, $blacklist, true ) ) {
                $blacklist[] = $ip;
                update_option( 'bap_blacklist_ips', implode( "\n", $blacklist ) );

                $duration_hours = (int) get_option( 'bap_auto_ban_duration', 24 );
                if ( $duration_hours > 0 ) {
                    $expirations        = get_option( 'bap_auto_ban_expirations', [] );
                    $expirations[ $ip ] = time() + ( $duration_hours * HOUR_IN_SECONDS );
                    update_option( 'bap_auto_ban_expirations', $expirations, false );
                }
            }
            delete_transient( $key );
        }
    }

    if ( function_exists( 'bap_queue_or_send_notification' ) ) {
        bap_queue_or_send_notification( $ip, $country, $reason );
    }
}

add_action( 'bap_daily_logs_cleanup', function() {
    $days = (int) get_option( 'bap_logs_retention_days', 30 );
    if ( $days <= 0 ) {
        return;
    }
    global $wpdb;
    $table  = bap_get_logs_table_name();
    $cutoff = gmdate( 'Y-m-d H:i:s', time() - ( $days * DAY_IN_SECONDS ) );
    $wpdb->query( $wpdb->prepare( "DELETE FROM `{$table}` WHERE created_at < %s", $cutoff ) );
} );

if ( ! wp_next_scheduled( 'bap_daily_logs_cleanup' ) ) {
    wp_schedule_event( time() + HOUR_IN_SECONDS, 'daily', 'bap_daily_logs_cleanup' );
}

<?php
if ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) {
    exit;
}

class BAP_MMDB_Reader {
    private $fh;
    private $file_size;
    private $node_count;
    private $record_size;
    private $ip_version;
    private $node_byte_size;
    private $search_tree_size;
    private $data_section_start;

    public function __construct( $file_path ) {
        if ( ! is_readable( $file_path ) ) {
            throw new Exception( 'Archivo MMDB no legible' );
        }
        $this->file_size = filesize( $file_path );
        $this->fh        = fopen( $file_path, 'rb' );
        if ( ! $this->fh ) {
            throw new Exception( 'No se pudo abrir MMDB' );
        }

        $marker    = hex2bin( 'abcdef' ) . 'MaxMind.com';
        $read_size = min( $this->file_size, 131072 );
        fseek( $this->fh, $this->file_size - $read_size );
        $tail = fread( $this->fh, $read_size );
        $pos  = strrpos( $tail, $marker );
        if ( false === $pos ) {
            throw new Exception( 'Metadatos MMDB no encontrados' );
        }

        $meta_res                 = $this->decode( ( $this->file_size - $read_size + $pos ) + strlen( $marker ) );
        $metadata                 = reset( $meta_res );
        $this->node_count         = (int) $metadata['node_count'];
        $this->record_size        = (int) $metadata['record_size'];
        $this->ip_version         = (int) $metadata['ip_version'];
        $this->node_byte_size     = (int) ( $this->record_size / 4 );
        $this->search_tree_size   = $this->node_count * $this->node_byte_size;
        $this->data_section_start = $this->search_tree_size + 16;
    }

    public function __destruct() {
        if ( is_resource( $this->fh ) ) {
            fclose( $this->fh );
        }
    }

    public function lookup_country( $ip ) {
        $packed = @inet_pton( $ip );
        if ( false === $packed ) {
            return '';
        }
        if ( 4 === strlen( $packed ) && 6 === $this->ip_version ) {
            $packed = str_repeat( "\0", 12 ) . $packed;
        }

        $bit_count = strlen( $packed ) * 8;
        $node      = 0;

        for ( $i = 0; $i < $bit_count; $i++ ) {
            if ( $node >= $this->node_count ) {
                break;
            }
            $byte = ord( substr( $packed, $i >> 3, 1 ) );
            $bit  = 1 & ( $byte >> ( 7 - ( $i % 8 ) ) );
            $node = $this->read_node( $node, $bit );
        }

        if ( $node > $this->node_count ) {
            $offset = ( $node - $this->node_count ) - 16 + $this->data_section_start;
            $res    = $this->decode( $offset );
            $record = reset( $res );
            return $record['country']['iso_code'] ?? ( $record['registered_country']['iso_code'] ?? '' );
        }
        return '';
    }

    public function lookup_asn( $ip ) {
        $packed = @inet_pton( $ip );
        if ( false === $packed ) {
            return '';
        }
        if ( 4 === strlen( $packed ) && 6 === $this->ip_version ) {
            $packed = str_repeat( "\0", 12 ) . $packed;
        }

        $bit_count = strlen( $packed ) * 8;
        $node      = 0;

        for ( $i = 0; $i < $bit_count; $i++ ) {
            if ( $node >= $this->node_count ) {
                break;
            }
            $byte = ord( substr( $packed, $i >> 3, 1 ) );
            $bit  = 1 & ( $byte >> ( 7 - ( $i % 8 ) ) );
            $node = $this->read_node( $node, $bit );
        }

        if ( $node > $this->node_count ) {
            $offset = ( $node - $this->node_count ) - 16 + $this->data_section_start;
            $res    = $this->decode( $offset );
            $record = reset( $res );
            $num    = $record['autonomous_system_number'] ?? '';
            $org    = $record['autonomous_system_organization'] ?? '';
            if ( '' !== $num ) {
                return 'AS' . $num . ( '' !== $org ? ' â€” ' . $org : '' );
            }
        }
        return '';
    }

    private function read_node( $node_number, $index ) {
        fseek( $this->fh, $node_number * $this->node_byte_size );
        $bytes = fread( $this->fh, $this->node_byte_size );

        if ( 24 === $this->record_size ) {
            $sub = ( 0 === $index ) ? substr( $bytes, 0, 3 ) : substr( $bytes, 3, 3 );
            $unp = unpack( 'N', "\0" . $sub );
            return (int) reset( $unp );
        }
        if ( 28 === $this->record_size ) {
            $mid = ord( substr( $bytes, 3, 1 ) );
            if ( 0 === $index ) {
                $sub = chr( ( $mid & 0xF0 ) >> 4 ) . substr( $bytes, 0, 3 );
            } else {
                $sub = chr( $mid & 0x0F ) . substr( $bytes, 4, 3 );
            }
            $unp = unpack( 'N', $sub );
            return (int) reset( $unp );
        }
        if ( 32 === $this->record_size ) {
            $sub = ( 0 === $index ) ? substr( $bytes, 0, 4 ) : substr( $bytes, 4, 4 );
            $unp = unpack( 'N', $sub );
            return (int) reset( $unp );
        }
        return 0;
    }

    private function decode( $offset ) {
        fseek( $this->fh, $offset );
        $ctrl = ord( fread( $this->fh, 1 ) );
        $type = $ctrl >> 5;

        if ( 1 === $type ) {
            $size     = ( ( $ctrl >> 3 ) & 0x3 ) + 1;
            $val_bits = $ctrl & 0x7;
            $extra    = ( $size < 4 ) ? fread( $this->fh, $size ) : fread( $this->fh, 3 );
            $next_pos = ftell( $this->fh );
            $packed   = str_pad( ( $size < 4 ) ? chr( $val_bits ) . $extra : $extra, 4, "\0", STR_PAD_LEFT );
            $unp      = unpack( 'N', $packed );
            $ptr      = (int) reset( $unp );
            $offsets  = [ 1 => 0, 2 => 2048, 3 => 526336, 4 => 0 ];
            $res      = $this->decode( $ptr + $offsets[ $size ] + $this->data_section_start );
            return [ reset( $res ), $next_pos ];
        }

        if ( 0 === $type ) {
            $type = 7 + ord( fread( $this->fh, 1 ) );
        }

        $size = $ctrl & 0x1F;
        if ( $size >= 29 ) {
            $raw = fread( $this->fh, $size - 28 );
            if ( 29 === $size ) {
                $size = 29 + ord( $raw );
            } elseif ( 30 === $size ) {
                $unp  = unpack( 'n', $raw );
                $size = 285 + (int) reset( $unp );
            } elseif ( 31 === $size ) {
                $unp  = unpack( 'N', "\0" . $raw );
                $size = 65821 + (int) reset( $unp );
            }
        }

        if ( 2 === $type || 4 === $type ) {
            return [ ( $size > 0 ) ? fread( $this->fh, $size ) : '', ftell( $this->fh ) ];
        }
        if ( 5 === $type || 6 === $type || 8 === $type ) {
            $raw = ( $size > 0 ) ? fread( $this->fh, $size ) : '';
            $unp = unpack( 'N', str_pad( $raw, 4, "\0", STR_PAD_LEFT ) );
            return [ (int) reset( $unp ), ftell( $this->fh ) ];
        }
        if ( 7 === $type ) {
            $map = [];
            $pos = ftell( $this->fh );
            for ( $i = 0; $i < $size; $i++ ) {
                $k_res                 = $this->decode( $pos );
                $v_res                 = $this->decode( end( $k_res ) );
                $map[ reset( $k_res ) ] = reset( $v_res );
                $pos                   = end( $v_res );
            }
            return [ $map, $pos ];
        }
        if ( 11 === $type ) {
            $arr = [];
            $pos = ftell( $this->fh );
            for ( $i = 0; $i < $size; $i++ ) {
                $v_res = $this->decode( $pos );
                $arr[] = reset( $v_res );
                $pos   = end( $v_res );
            }
            return [ $arr, $pos ];
        }
        if ( 14 === $type ) {
            return [ (bool) $size, ftell( $this->fh ) ];
        }
        if ( 3 === $type ) {
            $unp = unpack( 'E', fread( $this->fh, 8 ) );
            return [ reset( $unp ), ftell( $this->fh ) ];
        }
        if ( 15 === $type ) {
            $unp = unpack( 'G', fread( $this->fh, 4 ) );
            return [ reset( $unp ), ftell( $this->fh ) ];
        }
        $raw = ( $size > 0 ) ? fread( $this->fh, $size ) : '';
        return [ bin2hex( $raw ), ftell( $this->fh ) ];
    }
}

add_filter( 'cron_schedules', function( $schedules ) {
    $schedules['bap_6hours']  = [ 'interval' => 6 * HOUR_IN_SECONDS, 'display' => 'Cada 6 horas' ];
    $schedules['bap_12hours'] = [ 'interval' => 12 * HOUR_IN_SECONDS, 'display' => 'Cada 12 horas' ];
    $schedules['bap_2weeks']  = [ 'interval' => 14 * DAY_IN_SECONDS, 'display' => 'Cada 2 semanas' ];
    $schedules['bap_monthly'] = [ 'interval' => 30 * DAY_IN_SECONDS, 'display' => 'Cada mes' ];
    return $schedules;
});

function bap_reschedule_mail_cron( $freq ) {
    wp_clear_scheduled_hook( 'bap_send_digest_email_event' );
    $map = [
        'hourly'  => 'hourly',
        '6hours'  => 'bap_6hours',
        '12hours' => 'bap_12hours',
        'daily'   => 'daily',
        'weekly'  => 'weekly',
        '2weeks'  => 'bap_2weeks',
        'monthly' => 'bap_monthly',
    ];
    if ( isset( $map[ $freq ] ) ) {
        $recurrence = $map[ $freq ];
        $intervals  = [
            'hourly'      => HOUR_IN_SECONDS,
            'bap_6hours'  => 6 * HOUR_IN_SECONDS,
            'bap_12hours' => 12 * HOUR_IN_SECONDS,
            'daily'       => DAY_IN_SECONDS,
            'weekly'      => WEEK_IN_SECONDS,
            'bap_2weeks'  => 14 * DAY_IN_SECONDS,
            'bap_monthly' => 30 * DAY_IN_SECONDS,
        ];
        wp_schedule_event( time() + $intervals[ $recurrence ], $recurrence, 'bap_send_digest_email_event' );
    }
}

add_action( 'update_option_bap_notify_freq', function( $old_val, $new_val ) {
    bap_reschedule_mail_cron( $new_val );
}, 10, 2 );

add_action( 'bap_send_digest_email_event', 'bap_flush_email_digest' );

function bap_get_notify_recipients() {
    $raw = trim( (string) get_option( 'bap_notify_emails', '' ) );
    if ( '' === $raw ) {
        return [ get_option( 'admin_email' ) ];
    }
    $valid = [];
    foreach ( preg_split( '/[\r\n,;]+/', $raw ) as $mail ) {
        $m = sanitize_email( trim( $mail ) );
        if ( is_email( $m ) ) {
            $valid[] = $m;
        }
    }
    return ! empty( $valid ) ? array_unique( $valid ) : [ get_option( 'admin_email' ) ];
}

function bap_queue_or_send_notification( $ip, $country, $reason ) {
    $freq = (string) get_option( 'bap_notify_freq', 'none' );
    if ( 'none' === $freq || '' === $freq ) {
        return;
    }

    $asn  = function_exists( 'bap_get_asn_by_ip' ) ? bap_get_asn_by_ip( $ip ) : 'N/D';
    $risk = function_exists( 'bap_get_abuseipdb_score' ) ? bap_get_abuseipdb_score( $ip ) : null;
    $item = [
        'time'    => current_time( 'mysql' ),
        'ip'      => $ip,
        'country' => $country,
        'asn'     => $asn,
        'risk'    => ( null === $risk ) ? 'N/D' : $risk . '%',
        'reason'  => $reason,
        'uri'     => isset( $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] ) ? sanitize_text_field( wp_unslash( $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] ) ) : '',
    ];

    if ( 'instant' === $freq ) {
        $site    = wp_specialchars_decode( get_bloginfo( 'name' ), ENT_QUOTES );
        $subject = '[' . $site . '] Intento bloqueado: ' . $ip . ' (' . $country . ')';
        $body    = "Se ha bloqueado un intento de acceso al panel de administraciÃ³n:\n\n"
                 . "Fecha/Hora: {$item['time']}\n"
                 . "IP: {$item['ip']}\n"
                 . "PaÃ­s: {$item['country']}\n"
                 . "ASN / Proveedor: {$item['asn']}\n"
                 . "Riesgo AbuseIPDB: {$item['risk']}\n"
                 . "Motivo: {$item['reason']}\n"
                 . "URL solicitada: {$item['uri']}\n";
        wp_mail( bap_get_notify_recipients(), $subject, $body );
        return;
    }

    $queue = get_option( 'bap_email_digest_queue', [] );
    if ( ! is_array( $queue ) ) {
        $queue = [];
    }
    $queue[] = $item;
    if ( count( $queue ) > 2000 ) {
        $queue = array_slice( $queue, -2000 );
    }
    update_option( 'bap_email_digest_queue', $queue, false );

    if ( ! wp_next_scheduled( 'bap_send_digest_email_event' ) ) {
        bap_reschedule_mail_cron( $freq );
    }
}

function bap_flush_email_digest() {
    $queue = get_option( 'bap_email_digest_queue', [] );
    if ( empty( $queue ) || ! is_array( $queue ) ) {
        return;
    }
    delete_option( 'bap_email_digest_queue' );

    $site    = wp_specialchars_decode( get_bloginfo( 'name' ), ENT_QUOTES );
    $total   = count( $queue );
    $subject = '[' . $site . '] Resumen de seguridad: ' . $total . ' intentos bloqueados';
    $body    = bap_render_html_digest_email( $queue, false );
    $headers = [ 'Content-Type: text/html; charset=UTF-8' ];

    wp_mail( bap_get_notify_recipients(), $subject, $body, $headers );
}

function bap_build_single_condition_check( $key, $ip, $country, array $profile, $auto_ban_abuse ) {
    if ( 'tor' === $key ) {
        if ( '1' !== (string) ( $profile['block_tor'] ?? '0' ) || ! function_exists( 'bap_is_tor_exit_node' ) ) {
            return null;
        }
        return [
            'pass'   => ! bap_is_tor_exit_node( $ip ),
            'reason' => 'Nodo de salida Tor',
        ];
    }

    if ( 'firehol' === $key ) {
        if ( '1' !== (string) ( $profile['block_firehol'] ?? '0' ) || ! function_exists( 'bap_is_in_firehol' ) ) {
            return null;
        }
        $cidr_hit = bap_is_in_firehol( $ip );
        return [
            'pass'   => ( false === $cidr_hit ),
            'reason' => "Red maliciosa FireHOL ({$cidr_hit})",
        ];
    }

    if ( 'abuse' === $key ) {
        $min_risk = (int) ( $profile['abuse_risk'] ?? 0 );
        if ( $min_risk <= 0 || ! function_exists( 'bap_get_abuseipdb_score' ) ) {
            return null;
        }
        $score = bap_get_abuseipdb_score( $ip );
        $fail  = ( null !== $score && $score >= $min_risk );
        if ( $fail && $auto_ban_abuse ) {
            $blacklist = bap_parse_list( get_option( 'bap_blacklist_ips', '' ), false );
            if ( ! in_array( $ip, $blacklist, true ) ) {
                $blacklist[] = $ip;
                update_option( 'bap_blacklist_ips', implode( "\n", $blacklist ) );

                $duration_hours = (int) get_option( 'bap_auto_ban_duration', 24 );
                if ( $duration_hours > 0 ) {
                    $expirations        = get_option( 'bap_auto_ban_expirations', [] );
                    $expirations[ $ip ] = time() + ( $duration_hours * HOUR_IN_SECONDS );
                    update_option( 'bap_auto_ban_expirations', $expirations, false );
                }
            }
        }
        return [
            'pass'   => ! $fail,
            'reason' => "Riesgo AbuseIPDB ({$score}% >= {$min_risk}%)",
        ];
    }

    if ( 'country' === $key ) {
        $ctry_list = bap_parse_list( $profile['countries'] ?? '', true );
        if ( empty( $ctry_list ) ) {
            return null;
        }
        $ctry_mode = (string) ( $profile['country_mode'] ?? 'allow' );
        $in_ctry   = in_array( $country, $ctry_list, true );
        return [
            'pass'   => ( 'allow' === $ctry_mode ) ? $in_ctry : ! $in_ctry,
            'reason' => ( 'allow' === $ctry_mode ) ? "PaÃ­s no permitido ({$country})" : "PaÃ­s bloqueado ({$country})",
        ];
    }

    if ( 'asn' === $key ) {
        $asn_list = bap_parse_list( $profile['asns'] ?? '', true );
        if ( empty( $asn_list ) || ! function_exists( 'bap_get_asn_by_ip' ) ) {
            return null;
        }
        $asn_mode  = (string) ( $profile['asn_mode'] ?? 'block' );
        $asn_code  = '';
        $asn_label = bap_get_asn_by_ip( $ip, $asn_code );
        $in_asn    = ( '' !== $asn_code && in_array( $asn_code, $asn_list, true ) );
        return [
            'pass'   => ( 'allow' === $asn_mode ) ? $in_asn : ! $in_asn,
            'reason' => ( 'allow' === $asn_mode ) ? 'ASN no autorizado (' . ( $asn_label ?: 'N/D' ) . ')' : "ASN bloqueado ({$asn_label})",
        ];
    }

    return null;
}

function bap_evaluate_rule_profile( $ip, $country, array $profile, $auto_ban_abuse = false, $ok_msg = 'Cumple las reglas' ) {
    if ( bap_ip_in_list( $ip, bap_parse_list( get_option( 'bap_blacklist_ips', '' ) ) ) ) {
        return [ 'status' => 'block', 'reason' => 'IP/Rango en lista negra' ];
    }

    $norm   = bap_normalize_web_rule( $profile );
    $order  = $norm['order'];
    $ops    = $norm['ops'];
    $checks = [];

    foreach ( $order as $pos => $key ) {
        $item = bap_build_single_condition_check( $key, $ip, $country, $norm, $auto_ban_abuse );
        if ( null !== $item ) {
            $item['op'] = ( $pos > 0 ) ? ( $ops[ $pos - 1 ] ?? 'AND' ) : null;
            $checks[]   = $item;
        }
    }

    if ( empty( $checks ) ) {
        return [ 'status' => 'allow', 'reason' => $ok_msg ];
    }

    $acc_pass    = $checks[0]['pass'];
    $last_reason = $checks[0]['pass'] ? '' : $checks[0]['reason'];

    for ( $i = 1, $total = count( $checks ); $i < $total; $i++ ) {
        $step = $checks[ $i ];
        if ( 'OR' === $step['op'] ) {
            $acc_pass = ( $acc_pass || $step['pass'] );
            if ( ! $acc_pass && ! $step['pass'] ) {
                $last_reason = $step['reason'];
            }
        } else {
            if ( $acc_pass && ! $step['pass'] ) {
                $last_reason = $step['reason'];
            }
            $acc_pass = ( $acc_pass && $step['pass'] );
        }
    }

    if ( ! $acc_pass ) {
        return [
            'status' => 'block',
            'reason' => '' !== $last_reason ? $last_reason : 'No cumple la combinaciÃ³n de filtros Y/O',
        ];
    }

    return [ 'status' => 'allow', 'reason' => $ok_msg ];
}

function bap_evaluate_ip_access( $ip, $country, $context = 'admin', $auto_ban_abuse = false, $uri = '' ) {
    if ( 'web' === $context ) {
        $matched = bap_match_web_rule_for_uri( $uri );
        $rule    = $matched['rule'];
        $label   = ( '__all__' === $matched['scope'] ) ? 'Global' : $matched['scope'];

        if ( '1' !== (string) ( $rule['enabled'] ?? '0' ) ) {
            return [
                'status' => 'allow',
                'reason' => 'Sin bloqueo activo para [' . $label . ']',
                'scope'  => $matched['scope'],
            ];
        }

        $res          = bap_evaluate_rule_profile( $ip, $country, $rule, false, 'Cumple reglas Web [' . $label . ']' );
        $res['scope'] = $matched['scope'];
        return $res;
    }

    $c_mode  = (string) get_option( 'bap_country_mode', 'allow' );
    $a_mode  = (string) get_option( 'bap_asn_mode', 'allow' );
    $profile = [
        'order'         => [ 'tor', 'firehol', 'abuse', 'country', 'asn' ],
        'ops'           => [ 'AND', 'AND', 'AND', 'AND' ],
        'country_mode'  => $c_mode,
        'countries'     => (string) get_option( 'block' === $c_mode ? 'bap_blocked_countries' : 'bap_allowed_countries', 'allow' === $c_mode ? 'ES' : '' ),
        'asn_mode'      => $a_mode,
        'asns'          => (string) get_option( 'block' === $a_mode ? 'bap_blocked_asns' : 'bap_allowed_asns', '' ),
        'abuse_risk'    => (int) get_option( 'bap_abuseipdb_min_risk', 90 ),
        'block_tor'     => (string) get_option( 'bap_block_tor', '1' ),
        'block_firehol' => (string) get_option( 'bap_block_firehol', '1' ),
    ];

    return bap_evaluate_rule_profile( $ip, $country, $profile, $auto_ban_abuse, 'Cumple todas las reglas de acceso al Panel Admin' );
}

function bap_get_tor_list_path() {
    return bap_get_geo_storage_path( 'tor-exits.json' );

function bap_get_firehol_list_path() {
    return bap_get_geo_storage_path( 'firehol-l1.json' );

function bap_install_tor_from_content( $content ) {
    $ips = [];
    foreach ( preg_split( '/\r\n|\r|\n/', (string) $content ) as $line ) {
        $ip = trim( strtok( $line, " \t#" ) );
        if ( filter_var( $ip, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP ) ) {
            $ips[ $ip ] = 1;
        }
    }
    if ( empty( $ips ) ) {
        return new WP_Error( 'tor_empty', 'No se encontraron IPs vÃ¡lidas de nodos Tor.' );
    }
    return bap_save_json_list( 'tor-exits.json', $ips, 'bap_tor_last_updated', 'bap_tor_count' );
}

function bap_download_tor_database() {
    $urls = [
        'https://' . implode( '.', [ 'check', 'torproject', 'org' ] ) . '/torbulkexitlist',
        'https://' . implode( '.', [ 'raw', 'githubusercontent', 'com' ] ) . '/' . implode( '/', [ 'SecOps-Institute', 'Tor-IP-Addresses', 'master', 'tor-exit-nodes.lst' ] ),
    ];
    foreach ( $urls as $url ) {
        $res = wp_remote_get( $url, [ 'timeout' => 25 ] );
        if ( ! is_wp_error( $res ) && 200 === wp_remote_retrieve_response_code( $res ) ) {
            $ok = bap_install_tor_from_content( wp_remote_retrieve_body( $res ) );
            if ( true === $ok ) {
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return new WP_Error( 'tor_dl', 'No se pudo descargar la lista de nodos Tor.' );
}

function bap_install_firehol_from_content( $content ) {
    $cidrs = [];
    foreach ( preg_split( '/\r\n|\r|\n/', (string) $content ) as $line ) {
        $item = trim( strtok( $line, " \t#" ) );
        if ( '' === $item ) {
            continue;
        }
        if ( false === strpos( $item, '/' ) && filter_var( $item, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP, FILTER_FLAG_IPV4 ) ) {
            $item .= '/32';
        }
        if ( preg_match( '/^([0-9.]+)\/([0-9]{1,2})$/', $item, $m ) && filter_var( $m[1], FILTER_VALIDATE_IP, FILTER_FLAG_IPV4 ) ) {
            $prefix = (int) $m[2];
            if ( $prefix >= 8 && $prefix <= 32 ) {
                $cidrs[] = $item;
            }
        }
    }
    $cidrs = array_values( array_unique( $cidrs ) );
    if ( empty( $cidrs ) ) {
        return new WP_Error( 'fh_empty', 'No se encontraron rangos CIDR vÃ¡lidos en el archivo.' );
    }
    return bap_save_json_list( 'firehol-l1.json', $cidrs, 'bap_firehol_last_updated', 'bap_firehol_count' );
}

function bap_download_firehol_database() {
    $urls = [
        'https://' . implode( '.', [ 'raw', 'githubusercontent', 'com' ] ) . '/' . implode( '/', [ 'firehol', 'blocklist-ipsets', 'master', 'firehol_level1.netset' ] ),
        'https://' . implode( '.', [ 'iplists', 'firehol', 'org' ] ) . '/files/firehol_level1.netset',
    ];
    foreach ( $urls as $url ) {
        $res = wp_remote_get( $url, [ 'timeout' => 30 ] );
        if ( ! is_wp_error( $res ) && 200 === wp_remote_retrieve_response_code( $res ) ) {
            $ok = bap_install_firehol_from_content( wp_remote_retrieve_body( $res ) );
            if ( true === $ok ) {
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return new WP_Error( 'fh_dl', 'No se pudo descargar la lista FireHOL Level 1.' );
}

function bap_is_tor_exit_node( $ip ) {
    static $tor_db = null;
    if ( null === $tor_db ) {
        $f      = bap_get_tor_list_path();
        $tor_db = file_exists( $f ) ? json_decode( (string) file_get_contents( $f ), true ) : [];
        if ( ! is_array( $tor_db ) ) {
            $tor_db = [];
        }
    }
    return isset( $tor_db[ $ip ] );
}

function bap_is_in_firehol( $ip ) {
    if ( ! filter_var( $ip, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP, FILTER_FLAG_IPV4 ) ) {
        return false;
    }
    static $cidrs = null;
    if ( null === $cidrs ) {
        $f     = bap_get_firehol_list_path();
        $cidrs = file_exists( $f ) ? json_decode( (string) file_get_contents( $f ), true ) : [];
        if ( ! is_array( $cidrs ) ) {
            $cidrs = [];
        }
    }
    if ( empty( $cidrs ) ) {
        return false;
    }
    $ip_long = ip2long( $ip );
    foreach ( $cidrs as $cidr ) {
        list( $subnet, $bits ) = explode( '/', $cidr );
        $mask = -1 << ( 32 - (int) $bits );
        if ( ( $ip_long & $mask ) === ( ip2long( $subnet ) & $mask ) ) {
            return $cidr;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

<?php
if ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) {

function bap_get_default_web_rule( $enabled = '0' ) {
    return [
        'enabled'       => $enabled,
        'order'         => [ 'tor', 'firehol', 'abuse', 'country', 'asn' ],
        'ops'           => [ 'AND', 'AND', 'AND', 'AND' ],
        'country_mode'  => 'allow',
        'countries'     => '',
        'asn_mode'      => 'block',
        'asns'          => '',
        'abuse_risk'    => 0,
        'block_tor'     => '0',
        'block_firehol' => '0',
        'redirect_url'  => '',
        'block_message' => '',
    ];
}

function bap_normalize_web_rule( array $rule ) {
    $def   = bap_get_default_web_rule( '0' );
    $valid = [ 'tor', 'firehol', 'abuse', 'country', 'asn' ];
    $order = [];

    if ( ! empty( $rule['order'] ) && is_array( $rule['order'] ) ) {
        foreach ( $rule['order'] as $k ) {
            if ( in_array( $k, $valid, true ) && ! in_array( $k, $order, true ) ) {
                $order[] = $k;
            }
        }
    }
    foreach ( $valid as $k ) {
        if ( ! in_array( $k, $order, true ) ) {
            $order[] = $k;
        }
    }

    $ops = [];
    if ( ! empty( $rule['ops'] ) && is_array( $rule['ops'] ) ) {
        for ( $i = 0; $i < 4; $i++ ) {
            $ops[ $i ] = ( 'OR' === strtoupper( (string) ( $rule['ops'][ $i ] ?? 'AND' ) ) ) ? 'OR' : 'AND';
        }
    } else {
        $legacy = [ $rule['op_1'] ?? 'AND', $rule['op_2'] ?? 'AND', $rule['op_3'] ?? 'AND', $rule['op_4'] ?? 'AND' ];
        for ( $i = 0; $i < 4; $i++ ) {
            $ops[ $i ] = ( 'OR' === strtoupper( (string) $legacy[ $i ] ) ) ? 'OR' : 'AND';
        }
    }

    $merged          = wp_parse_args( $rule, $def );
    $merged['order'] = $order;
    $merged['ops']   = $ops;
    return $merged;
}

function bap_get_web_rules() {
    $rules = get_option( 'bap_web_rules', null );
    if ( is_array( $rules ) && isset( $rules['__all__'] ) ) {
        return $rules;
    }

    $old_enabled = (string) get_option( 'bap_web_enabled', '0' );
    $old_scope   = (string) get_option( 'bap_web_scope', 'all' );
    $old_cmode   = (string) get_option( 'bap_web_country_mode', 'allow' );
    $old_amode   = (string) get_option( 'bap_web_asn_mode', 'block' );

    $migrated = bap_normalize_web_rule( [
        'enabled'       => $old_enabled,
        'country_mode'  => $old_cmode,
        'countries'     => (string) get_option( 'block' === $old_cmode ? 'bap_web_blocked_countries' : 'bap_web_allowed_countries', '' ),
        'asn_mode'      => $old_amode,
        'asns'          => (string) get_option( 'block' === $old_amode ? 'bap_web_blocked_asns' : 'bap_web_allowed_asns', '' ),
        'abuse_risk'    => (int) get_option( 'bap_web_abuse_risk', 0 ),
        'block_tor'     => (string) get_option( 'bap_web_block_tor', '0' ),
        'block_firehol' => (string) get_option( 'bap_web_block_firehol', '0' ),
        'redirect_url'  => (string) get_option( 'bap_web_redirect_url', '' ),
        'block_message' => (string) get_option( 'bap_web_block_message', '' ),
    ] );

    $rules = [ '__all__' => bap_get_default_web_rule( '0' ) ];
    if ( 'paths' === $old_scope ) {
        foreach ( bap_parse_list( get_option( 'bap_web_paths', '' ), false ) as $p ) {
            $rules[ $p ] = $migrated;
        }
    } else {
        $rules['__all__'] = $migrated;
    }

    update_option( 'bap_web_rules', $rules );
    return $rules;
}

function bap_match_web_rule_for_uri( $uri, $path = '' ) {
    $rules = bap_get_web_rules();
    $uri   = strtolower( (string) $uri );
    $path  = '' !== $path ? strtolower( (string) $path ) : (string) wp_parse_url( $uri, PHP_URL_PATH );

    foreach ( $rules as $pattern => $rule ) {
        if ( '__all__' === $pattern ) {
            continue;
        }
        $regex = '#^' . str_replace( '\*', '.*', preg_quote( strtolower( trim( $pattern ) ), '#' ) ) . '$#i';
        if ( preg_match( $regex, $path ) || preg_match( $regex, $uri ) ) {
            return [
                'scope' => $pattern,
                'rule'  => bap_normalize_web_rule( (array) $rule ),
            ];
        }
    }

    return [
        'scope' => '__all__',
        'rule'  => bap_normalize_web_rule( (array) ( $rules['__all__'] ?? [] ) ),
    ];
}

function bap_render_custom_403_page( $title, $message, $ip = '', $country = '', $reason = '' ) {
    status_header( 403 );
    nocache_headers();
    header( 'Content-Type: text/html; charset=utf-8' );
    ?>
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html lang="es">
    <head>
        <meta charset="UTF-8" />
        <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
        <meta name="robots" content="noindex, nofollow" />
        <title>403 - <?php echo esc_html( $title ); ?></title>
        <style>
            * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
            body {
                min-height: 100vh;
                display: flex;
                align-items: center;
                justify-content: center;
                background: radial-gradient(circle at top, #1e293b 0%, #0f172a 100%);
                color: #f8fafc;
                font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, Oxygen, Ubuntu, sans-serif;
                padding: 20px;
            }
            .bap-card {
                width: 100%;
                max-width: 820px;
                background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.85);
                border: 1px solid #334155;
                border-radius: 16px;
                padding: 40px 32px;
                box-shadow: 0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6);
                display: flex;
                align-items: center;
                justify-content: space-between;

<?php
if ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) {
    exit;
}

function bap_parse_country_redirects( $raw ) {
    $map = [];
    if ( empty( $raw ) ) {
        return $map;
    }
    foreach ( preg_split( '/[\r\n]+/', (string) $raw ) as $line ) {
        $line = trim( $line );
        if ( '' === $line || 0 === strpos( $line, '#' ) ) {
            continue;
        }
        if ( preg_match( '/^([a-zA-Z]{2})\s*(?:=>|=|\s+)\s*(https?:\/\/\S+)$/i', $line, $m ) ) {
            $code = strtoupper( $m[1] );
            $url  = esc_url_raw( trim( $m[2] ) );
            if ( filter_var( $url, FILTER_VALIDATE_URL ) ) {
                $map[ $code ] = $url;
            }
        }
    }
    return $map;
}

function bap_sanitize_country_redirects( $raw ) {
    $map = bap_parse_country_redirects( $raw );
    $out = [];
    foreach ( $map as $code => $url ) {
        $out[] = $code . ' => ' . $url;
    }
    return implode( "\n", $out );
}

function bap_get_country_redirect_url( $country ) {
    $code = strtoupper( trim( (string) $country ) );
    if ( 2 !== strlen( $code ) ) {
        return '';
    }
    $map = bap_parse_country_redirects( get_option( 'bap_web_country_redirects', '' ) );
    if ( empty( $map[ $code ] ) ) {
        return '';
    }

    $target = $map[ $code ];
    // Evitar bucle si el usuario ya estÃ¡ en la URL de destino
    $req_uri  = isset( $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] ) ? wp_unslash( $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] ) : '';
    $cur_host = isset( $_SERVER['HTTP_HOST'] ) ? strtolower( wp_unslash( $_SERVER['HTTP_HOST'] ) ) : '';
    $t_host   = strtolower( (string) wp_parse_url( $target, PHP_URL_HOST ) );
    $t_path   = (string) wp_parse_url( $target, PHP_URL_PATH );
    $c_path   = (string) wp_parse_url( $req_uri, PHP_URL_PATH );

    if ( $cur_host === $t_host && rtrim( $c_path, '/' ) === rtrim( $t_path, '/' ) ) {
        return '';
    }

    return $target;
}

<?php
if ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) {

function bap_check_frontend_rules() {
    if ( is_user_logged_in() || wp_doing_ajax() || wp_doing_cron() ) {
        return;
    }
    if ( '1' === (string) get_option( 'bap_web_allow_seo_bots', '1' ) && function_exists( 'bap_is_verified_seo_bot' ) && bap_is_verified_seo_bot() ) {
        return;
    }

    $uri     = isset( $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] ) ? rawurldecode( wp_unslash( $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] ) ) : '/';
    $matched = bap_match_web_rule_for_uri( $uri );
    $rule    = $matched['rule'];

    if ( '1' !== (string) ( $rule['enabled'] ?? '0' ) ) {
        return;
    }

    $ip = bap_get_user_ip();
    if ( bap_is_server_ip( $ip ) || bap_ip_in_list( $ip, bap_parse_list( get_option( 'bap_whitelist_ips', '' ) ) ) ) {
        return;
    }

    $country = function_exists( 'bap_get_country_by_ip' ) ? bap_get_country_by_ip( $ip ) : 'DESCONOCIDO';

    if ( function_exists( 'bap_get_country_redirect_url' ) ) {
        $custom_redir = bap_get_country_redirect_url( $country );
        if ( '' !== $custom_redir ) {
            wp_redirect( esc_url_raw( $custom_redir ), 302 );
            exit;
        }
    }

    $verdict = bap_evaluate_rule_profile( $ip, $country, $rule, false );
    if ( 'block' === $verdict['status'] ) {
        $scope_tag = ( '__all__' === $matched['scope'] ) ? 'Web Global' : 'Web (' . $matched['scope'] . ')';
        $reason    = $verdict['reason'];
        bap_log_blocked_attempt( $ip, $country, $scope_tag . ': ' . $reason );

        // 1. Prioridad: URL de redirecciÃ³n propia de esta regla -> respaldo global
        $url = trim( (string) ( $rule['redirect_url'] ?? '' ) );
        if ( '' === $url ) {
            $url = trim( (string) get_option( 'bap_web_redirect_url', '' ) );
        }
        if ( '' !== $url && filter_var( $url, FILTER_VALIDATE_URL ) ) {
            wp_redirect( esc_url_raw( $url ), 302 );
            exit;
        }

        // 2. Prioridad: Mensaje 403 propio de esta regla -> respaldo global
        $msg = trim( (string) ( $rule['block_message'] ?? '' ) );
        if ( '' === $msg ) {
            $msg = (string) get_option( 'bap_web_block_message', 'Este contenido no estÃ¡ disponible en tu ubicaciÃ³n o red.' );
        }

        if ( function_exists( 'bap_render_custom_403_page' ) ) {
            bap_render_custom_403_page( 'Acceso restringido', $msg, $ip, $country, $reason );
        }
        wp_die( '<h1>Acceso restringido</h1><p>' . esc_html( $msg ) . '</p>', 'Acceso Restringido', [ 'response' => 403 ] );
    }
}

<?php
if ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) {
    exit;
}

function bap_is_verified_seo_bot( $ip = '' ) {
    if ( '' === $ip ) {
        $ip = bap_get_user_ip();
    }
    if ( ! filter_var( $ip, FILTER_VALIDATE_IP ) ) {
        return false;
    }

    $ua = isset( $_SERVER['HTTP_USER_AGENT'] ) ? strtolower( sanitize_text_field( wp_unslash( $_SERVER['HTTP_USER_AGENT'] ) ) ) : '';
    if ( '' === $ua ) {
        return false;
    }

    $allowed_bots = [
        'googlebot'   => [ '.googlebot.com', '.google.com' ],
        'bingbot'     => [ '.search.msn.com' ],
        'duckduckbot' => [ '.duckduckgo.com' ],
        'applebot'    => [ '.applebot.apple.com' ],
    ];

    $matched_suffixes = [];
    foreach ( $allowed_bots as $token => $suffixes ) {
        if ( false !== strpos( $ua, $token ) ) {
            $matched_suffixes = $suffixes;
            break;
        }
    }

    if ( empty( $matched_suffixes ) ) {
        return false;
    }

    $cache_key = 'bap_seo_bot_' . md5( $ip );
    $cached    = get_transient( $cache_key );
    if ( false !== $cached ) {
        return '1' === $cached;
    }

    // 1. Reverse DNS (PTR)
    $host = @gethostbyaddr( $ip );
    if ( ! $host || $host === $ip ) {
        set_transient( $cache_key, '0', HOUR_IN_SECONDS );
        return false;
    }

    $host_lower   = strtolower( rtrim( $host, '.' ) );
    $valid_domain = false;
    foreach ( $matched_suffixes as $suffix ) {
        if ( substr( $host_lower, -strlen( $suffix ) ) === $suffix ) {
            $valid_domain = true;
            break;
        }
    }

    if ( ! $valid_domain ) {
        set_transient( $cache_key, '0', HOUR_IN_SECONDS );
        return false;
    }

    // 2. Forward DNS (FCrDNS) compatible con IPv4 (A) e IPv6 (AAAA)
    $resolved_ips = [];
    $records      = @dns_get_record( $host_lower, DNS_A + DNS_AAAA );
    if ( is_array( $records ) ) {
        foreach ( $records as $rec ) {
            if ( ! empty( $rec['ip'] ) ) {
                $resolved_ips[] = inet_ntop( inet_pton( $rec['ip'] ) );
            } elseif ( ! empty( $rec['ipv6'] ) ) {
                $resolved_ips[] = inet_ntop( inet_pton( $rec['ipv6'] ) );
            }
        }
    }

    $norm_ip  = inet_ntop( inet_pton( $ip ) );
    $verified = in_array( $norm_ip, $resolved_ips, true );

    set_transient( $cache_key, $verified ? '1' : '0', DAY_IN_SECONDS );
    return $verified;
}
